[SYSTEM]
You are an RTL (Register Transfer Level) design agent. Your task is to create a Verilog/SystemVerilog design that satisfies the given specification, is functionally correct, and has minimal cost (area_delay_product).

**Cost metric `area_delay_product`:** area × achieved critical-path delay — the classic area-delay product. Does NOT include simulation cycle count (contrast with the 'area_runtime_product' metric). Cost is measured on your design alone. The testbench and everything it owns (checking logic, the external memories, etc.) is not synthesized and contributes nothing to area, delay, power, or energy.

## Specification
Design a module named fp_mul_e5f10 that multiplies two floating-point numbers. Format: 16-bit (1 sign + 5 exponent + 10 fraction). Inputs: a and b (16-bit each). Output: y (16-bit). Subnormal numbers must be supported.

Required behaviour, bit-exact: subnormal inputs are used at their true value; results are rounded to nearest, ties to even, including results in the subnormal range; a NaN input and inf x 0 give the canonical quiet NaN 16'h7E00; inf x finite gives inf with the product's sign; an overflowing product gives inf; a zero product gives a signed zero. The testbench enumerates about 18.9 million operand pairs against a reference implementation.

A working starting point is provided: `starting_point.v` in your workspace, a plain readable implementation (unpack, significand multiply, normalise, round, pack) that is correct. Optimize from there.

## Tools
- create_file: Create a new file in your working directory
- replace_file: Replace the entire contents of an existing file
- apply_diff: Apply a unified diff to modify a file
- edit_file: Edit a file by replacing an exact string match with new content. Provide old_string (must match exactly once) and new_string.
- ls: List files in your working directory
- read_file: Read the contents of a file
- run_evaluation(filename[, target_delay]): Run evaluation on the given design file. Returns correctness (Verilator simulation) + cost (Yosys area_delay_product). The filename must be your main design file (e.g. 'design.sv'). Optionally pass target_delay (ps) to override the synthesis timing constraint.

## Strategy
1. Lay out an action plan. Try to cover a diverse set of approaches in your plan to increase the chances of finding a good solution within the step limit. Also once you find a new best solution, explore close solutions. Trade off exploration with exploitation.
2. First, create a simple, straightforward design that is functionally correct. Focus on getting 100% correctness first.
3. Run evaluation to confirm correctness and get the initial cost (area_delay_product).
4. ONLY after achieving 100% correctness, try to optimize the design to reduce the area_delay_product.
5. After each optimization, run evaluation to verify correctness is maintained and check the new cost.
6. If an optimization breaks correctness, revert and try a different approach.
7. Keep iterating until you run out of steps — use every step to try improvements. The run ends automatically at the step limit; your best passing design is kept.

In general, be creative and take risk. At least after you have a correct design, try bold optimizations to reduce area_delay_product, even if they might break correctness. You can always revert if it doesn't work.
The best results you obtain which is 100% correctness counts.
Try to beat at least the straightforward implementation.

Running evaluation is crucial after every change to ensure correctness and track cost. Do not skip evaluations.

You may do some 'thinking' alongside with your tool calling responses to explain your reasoning, but always remember to call a tool in every response.

## Important
- Always call a tool in every response.
- You have a maximum of **60 steps** (LLM turns). Budget them wisely: get a correct design first, then optimise. You may batch tool calls in one response --- at most 8, and only one `run_evaluation`.
- The best result is the one with lowest cost among designs that pass 100% of testbench checks.
- Use different filenames for different design variants so you can retrieve them later.
- If context files (e.g. component source files) are in your workspace, you can modify them directly using edit_file or replace_file to optimize the design. Make targeted, incremental changes rather than rewriting files from scratch — small edits are less likely to introduce bugs.
- Write clean, synthesizable RTL code.
- You can pass `target_delay` (ps) to `run_evaluation` to try different synthesis timing constraints; experimenting with this may improve PPA results (lower target delay may reduce delay, higher target delay may reduce area/transistor count). target_delay must be > 0.
- Do not spend too many steps sweeping `target_delay` — basically if you want to minimize delay choose a low value,if you want to minimize area/transistor count choose a high value. That's it.A few targeted experiments are more productive than an exhaustive sweep.

## Delay Optimization Tips
- `target_delay` trades area against delay (a lower value pressures toward faster logic, a higher value lets Yosys re-map to smaller cells). Do not overly iterate on different target delays; often the RTL structure is the real lever, target_delay is only a minor secondary knob.
- After getting a correct design, try different accumulation orderings (sequential vs. tree) and compare results — Yosys synthesis can produce noticeably different delays (~10 ps) from structurally similar descriptions.
- For designs dominated by a multiply-accumulate chain, the critical path is the multiplier followed by the adder tree. Reducing the adder depth or splitting into parallel partial sums can help.

## Reference Implementations
The following are reference implementations for inspiration. Study them for patterns and techniques, but do not copy them directly — adapt ideas to your specific design.

### Parallel-prefix adder (ripple-carry / carry-lookahead) (Add.sv)
```systemverilog
// Copyright 2024 ETH Zurich and University of Bologna.
// Solderpad Hardware License, Version 0.51, see LICENSE for details.
// SPDX-License-Identifier: SHL-0.51
//
// Based on the work by Reto Zimmermann 1998 - ETH Zürich
// Originally written in VHDL, available under: 
// https://iis-people.ee.ethz.ch/~zimmi/arith_lib.html#library
//
// Authors:
// - Thomas Benz <tbenz@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Philippe Sauter <phsauter@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Paul Scheffler <paulsc@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
//
// Description :
// Binary adder using ripple-carry or parallel-prefix carry-lookahead logic.
// S = A+B

module Add #(
	parameter int              width = 8,             // word width
	parameter lau_pkg::speed_e speed = lau_pkg::FAST  // performance parameter
) (
	input  logic [width-1:0] A,  // operands
	input  logic [width-1:0] B,
	output logic [width-1:0] S   // sum
);

	// Function: Binary adder using parallel-prefix carry-lookahead logic.

	logic [width-1:0] GI, PI;  // prefix gen./prop. in
	logic [width-1:0] GO, PO;  // prefix gen./prop. out
	logic [width-1:0] PT;  // adder propagate temp

	// Internal signals for unsigned operands
	logic [width-1:0] Auns, Buns, Suns;

	// default ripple-carry adder as slow implementation
	if (speed == lau_pkg::SLOW) begin
		// type conversion: std_logic_vector -> unsigned
		assign Auns = A;
		assign Buns = B;

		// addition
		assign Suns = Auns + Buns;

		// type conversion: unsigned -> std_logic_vector
		assign S = Suns;
	end else begin
		// parallel-prefix adders as medium and fast implementations

		// calculate prefix input generate/propagate signals
		assign GI = A & B;
		assign PI = A | B;
		// calculate adder propagate signals (PT = A xor B)
		assign PT = ~GI & PI;

		// calculate prefix output generate/propagate signals
		PrefixAndOr #(
			.width(width),
			.speed(speed)
		) prefix (
			.GI(GI),
			.PI(PI),
			.GO(GO),
			.PO(PO)
		);

		// calculate sum bits
		assign S = PT ^ {GO[width-2:0], 1'b0};
	end
endmodule



module behavioural_Add #(
	parameter int              width = 8,             // word width
	parameter lau_pkg::speed_e speed = lau_pkg::FAST  // performance parameter
) (
	input  logic [width-1:0] A,  // operands
	input  logic [width-1:0] B,
	output logic [width-1:0] S   // sum
);
	assign S = A + B;
endmodule
```

### Prefix AND-OR structures (Sklansky, Brent-Kung, serial) (PrefixAndOr.sv)
```systemverilog
// Copyright 2024 ETH Zurich and University of Bologna.
// Solderpad Hardware License, Version 0.51, see LICENSE for details.
// SPDX-License-Identifier: SHL-0.51
//
// Based on the work by Reto Zimmermann 1998 - ETH Zürich
// Originally written in VHDL, available under: 
// https://iis-people.ee.ethz.ch/~zimmi/arith_lib.html#library
//
// Authors:
// - Thomas Benz <tbenz@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Philippe Sauter <phsauter@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Paul Scheffler <paulsc@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
//
// Description :
// Prefix structures of different depth (i.e. speed) for carry calculation
// in binary adders. Compute in m levels new generate/propagate signal pairs
// for always larger groups of bits. Generate signals of the last level correspond
// to carries in binary addition. Basic logic operations: AND-OR for generate
// signals, AND for propagate signals.

module PrefixAndOr #(
	parameter int              width = 8,             // word width
	parameter lau_pkg::speed_e speed = lau_pkg::FAST  // performance parameter
) (
	input  logic [width-1:0] GI,  // gen./prop. in
	input  logic [width-1:0] PI,  // gen./prop. in
	output logic [width-1:0] GO,  // gen./prop. out
	output logic [width-1:0] PO   // gen./prop. out
);

	// Constants
	localparam int n = width;  // prefix structure width
	localparam int m = $clog2(width);  // prefix structure depth

	// Sklansky parallel-prefix carry-lookahead structure
	if (speed == lau_pkg::FAST) begin : fastPrefix
		logic [(m+1)*n-1:0] GT, PT;  // gen./prop. temp
			assign GT[n-1:0] = GI;
			assign PT[n-1:0] = PI;
			for (genvar l = 1; l <= m; l++) begin : levels
				for (genvar k = 0; k < 2 ** (m - l); k++) begin : groups
					for (genvar i = 0; i < 2 ** (l - 1); i++) begin : bits
						// pass prop and gen to following nodes
						if ((k * 2 ** l + i) < n) begin : white
							assign GT[l*n+k*2**l+i] = GT[(l-1)*n+k*2**l+i];
							assign PT[l*n+k*2**l+i] = PT[(l-1)*n+k*2**l+i];
						end
						// calculate new propagate and generate
						if ((k * 2 ** l + 2 ** (l - 1) + i) < n) begin : black
							assign GT[l*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i] = 
												GT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i]
											  | (  PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i]
												 & GT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) - 1] );
							assign PT[l*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i] = 
													PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i]
												  & PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) - 1];
						end
					end
				end
			end
			assign GO = GT[(m+1)*n-1 : m*n];
			assign PO = PT[(m+1)*n-1 : m*n];
	end

	// Brent-Kung parallel-prefix carry-lookahead structure
	if (speed == lau_pkg::MEDIUM) begin : mediumPrefix
		logic [(2*m)*n -1:0] GT, PT;  // gen./prop. temp
		assign GT[n-1:0] = GI;
		assign PT[n-1:0] = PI;

		for (genvar l = 1; l <= m; l++) begin : levels1
			for (genvar k = 0; k < 2**(m-l); k++) begin : groups
				for (genvar i = 0; i < 2**l -1; i++) begin : bits
					if ((k* 2**l +i) < n) begin : white
						assign GT[l*n + k* 2**l +i] = GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l +i];
						assign PT[l*n + k* 2**l +i] = PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l +i];
					end // white
				end // bits
				if ((k* 2**l + 2**l -1) < n) begin : black
					assign GT[l*n + k* 2**l + 2**l -1] =
								GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l + 2**l - 1] |
							  | (  PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l + 2**l     -1] 
							     & GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l + 2**(l-1) -1]);
					assign PT[l*n + k* 2**l + 2**l -1] =
								PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**l     -1] 
							  & PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) -1];
				end // black
			end
		end // level1
		for (genvar l = m +1; l < 2*m; l++) begin : levels2
			for (genvar i = 0; i < 2**(2*m -l); i++) begin : bits
				if (i < n) begin : white
					assign GT[l*n +i] = GT[(l-1)*n +i];
					assign PT[l*n +i] = PT[(l-1)*n +i];
				end // white
			end // bits
			for (genvar k = 1; k < 2**(l-m); k++) begin : groups
				if (l < 2*m -1) begin : empty
					for (genvar i = 0; i < 2**(2*m -l -1) -1; i++) begin : bits
						if ((k* 2**(2*m -l) +i) < n) begin : white
							assign GT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
							assign PT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
						end // white
					end
				end // empty
				if ((k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l -1) -1) < n) begin : black
					assign GT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1] = 
								GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m-l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1]
							  | (  PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m-l) + 2**(2*m-l-1) -1] 
								 & GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m-l) -1] );
					assign PT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1] = 
								PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1]
							  & PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) -1];
				end // black
				for (genvar i = 2**(2*m -l-1); i < 2**(2*m -l); i++) begin : bits
					if ((k* 2**(2*m -l) +i) < n) begin : white
						assign GT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
						assign PT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
					end // white
				end
			end
		end // level2
		assign GO = GT[2*m*n -1 : (2*m -1) * n];
		assign PO = PT[2*m*n -1 : (2*m -1) * n];
	end  // Serial-prefix carry-lookahead structure
	else if (speed == lau_pkg::SLOW) begin : slowPrefix
		logic [n-1:0] GT, PT;  // gen./prop. temp
		assign GT[0] = GI[0];
		assign PT[0] = PI[0];
		
		for (genvar i = 1; i < n; i++) begin : bits
			assign GT[i] = GI[i] | (PI[i] & GT[i-1]);
			assign PT[i] = PI[i] & PT[i-1];
		end
		assign GO = GT;
		assign PO = PT;
	end

endmodule

```

[Step 2/60]
// fp_mul_e5f10 -- IEEE binary16 multiplier (1 sign, 5 exponent, 10 fraction bits) with subnormal support.

module fp_mul_e5f10 (a, b, y);
  input  [15:0] a;
  input  [15:0] b;
  output [15:0] y;

  localparam BIAS         = 15;
  localparam MAX_E        = 31;   // inf / NaN
  localparam MAX_FINITE_E = 30;
  localparam PROD_W       = 22;   // 11 x 11 bit significand product

  // 1. unpack
  wire       s_a = a[15];
  wire       s_b = b[15];
  wire [4:0] e_a = a[14:10];
  wire [4:0] e_b = b[14:10];
  wire [9:0] f_a = a[9:0];
  wire [9:0] f_b = b[9:0];

  // 2. classify
  wire e_a_zero = (e_a == 5'd0);
  wire e_b_zero = (e_b == 5'd0);
  wire e_a_ones = (e_a == MAX_E);
  wire e_b_ones = (e_b == MAX_E);
  wire zero_a   = e_a_zero & (f_a == 10'd0);
  wire zero_b   = e_b_zero & (f_b == 10'd0);
  wire nan_a    = e_a_ones & (f_a != 10'd0);
  wire nan_b    = e_b_ones & (f_b != 10'd0);
  wire inf_a    = e_a_ones & (f_a == 10'd0);
  wire inf_b    = e_b_ones & (f_b == 10'd0);

  wire nan_in  = nan_a | nan_b | (inf_a & zero_b) | (zero_a & inf_b);
  wire inf_in  = inf_a | inf_b;
  wire zero_in = zero_a | zero_b;
  wire s_y     = s_a ^ s_b;

  // 3. effective significands and exponents (subnormal: no hidden one, exponent 1)
  wire [10:0] m_a    = {~e_a_zero, f_a};
  wire [10:0] m_b    = {~e_b_zero, f_b};
  wire [4:0]  ea_eff = e_a_zero ? 5'd1 : e_a;
  wire [4:0]  eb_eff = e_b_zero ? 5'd1 : e_b;

  // 4. significand product, its leading-zero count (0 for a zero product), below[k] = |prod[k:0]
  wire [PROD_W-1:0] prod = m_a * m_b;

  reg [4:0] lz;
  integer   i;
  always @* begin
    lz = 5'd0;
    for (i = 0; i < PROD_W; i = i + 1)
      if (prod[i]) lz = 5'd21 - i[4:0];
  end

  wire [PROD_W-1:0] below;
  genvar k;
  generate
    for (k = 0; k < PROD_W; k = k + 1) begin : g_below
      assign below[k] = |prod[k:0];
    end
  endgenerate

  function bit_at;   // v[idx], 0 outside the vector
    input [PROD_W-1:0] v;
    input integer      idx;
    begin
      bit_at = (idx >= 0 && idx < PROD_W) ? v[idx] : 1'b0;
    end
  endfunction

  // 5. normalise and round a normal result: leading one onto bit 10
  wire              need_right = (lz <= 5'd11);
  wire [4:0]        sr         = 5'd11 - lz;   // right shift (wide product)
  wire [4:0]        sl         = lz - 5'd11;   // left shift (narrow product)
  wire [PROD_W-1:0] shifted    = need_right ? (prod >> sr) : (prod << sl);
  wire [10:0]       mant_pre   = shifted[10:0];

  wire guard    = need_right ? bit_at(prod,  sr - 1) : 1'b0;   // first bit shifted out
  wire sticky   = need_right ? bit_at(below, sr - 2) : 1'b0;   // any bit below the guard
  wire round_up = guard & (sticky | mant_pre[0]);              // round to nearest, ties to even

  wire [11:0] mant_round = {1'b0, mant_pre} + {11'd0, round_up};
  wire        carry      = mant_round[11];   // rounded up to a new leading one
  wire [10:0] mant_post  = carry ? mant_round[11:1] : mant_round[10:0];
  wire [9:0]  frac_norm  = mant_post[9:0];

  // 6. exponent of a normal result
  wire [7:0] exp_sum     = {3'd0, ea_eff} + {3'd0, eb_eff};
  wire [7:0] lhs         = exp_sum + 8'd1 + {7'd0, carry};
  wire [7:0] limit_under = BIAS + {3'd0, lz};
  wire [7:0] limit_over  = BIAS + MAX_FINITE_E + {3'd0, lz};
  wire       underflow   = (lhs <= limit_under);   // subnormal (or zero) result
  wire       overflow    = (lhs >  limit_over);    // past the largest finite number
  wire [7:0] e_norm      = lhs - limit_under;
  wire [4:0] exp_norm    = e_norm[4:0];

  // 7. subnormal result: shift the raw product onto the subnormal grid, round once
  integer           total_shift;
  reg [PROD_W-1:0]  sub_shifted;
  always @* begin
    total_shift = (BIAS + 10 + 1) - $signed({1'b0, exp_sum});
    sub_shifted = (total_shift >= 0 && total_shift < PROD_W) ? (prod >> total_shift) : {PROD_W{1'b0}};
  end
  wire [9:0]  frac_trunc = sub_shifted[9:0];
  wire        guard_s    = bit_at(prod,  total_shift - 1);
  wire        sticky_s   = bit_at(below, total_shift - 2);
  wire        round_s    = guard_s & (sticky_s | frac_trunc[0]);
  wire [10:0] frac_sum   = {1'b0, frac_trunc} + {10'd0, round_s};
  wire        carry_s    = frac_sum[10];   // rounded up to the smallest normal
  wire [9:0]  frac_sub   = frac_sum[9:0];
  wire [4:0]  exp_sub    = {4'd0, carry_s};

  // 8. pack
  wire is_nan      = nan_in;
  wire is_inf      = ~is_nan & (inf_in | (overflow & ~zero_in));
  wire is_sub      = ~is_nan & ~is_inf & underflow;
  wire sub_is_zero = is_sub & (exp_sub == 5'd0) & (frac_sub == 10'd0);
  wire is_zero     = ~is_nan & ~is_inf & (zero_in | sub_is_zero);

  wire [4:0] exp_y  = (is_nan | is_inf) ? 5'd31 : is_zero ? 5'd0 : is_sub ? exp_sub : exp_norm;
  wire [9:0] frac_y = is_nan ? 10'h200 : (is_inf | is_zero) ? 10'd0 : is_sub ? frac_sub : frac_norm;
  wire       sign_y = is_nan ? 1'b0 : s_y;

  assign y = {sign_y, exp_y, frac_y};
endmodule


[ASSISTANT]
  -> run_evaluation(filename='starting_point.v')

  -> create_file(filename='fp_mul_v1.v', content="// fp_mul_e5f10 -- optimized variant 1: priority-encode only normal top bits\nmodule fp_mul_e5f10 (a, b, y);\n  input  [15:0] a;\n  input  [15:0] b;\n  output [15:0] y;\n\n  localparam BIAS         = 15;\n  localparam MAX_E        = 31;\n  localparam MAX_FINITE_E = 30;\n  localparam PROD_W       = 22;\n\n  wire       s_a = a[15];\n  wire       s_b = b[15];\n  wire [4:0] e_a = a[14:10];\n  wire [4:0] e_b = b[14:10];\n  wire [9:0] f_a = a[9:0];\n  wire [9:0] f_b = b[9:0];\n\n  wire e_a_zero = (e_a == 5'd0);\n  wire e_b_zero = (e_b == 5'd0);\n  wire e_a_ones = (e_a == MAX_E);\n  wire e_b_ones = (e_b == MAX_E);\n  wire zero_a   = e_a_zero & (f_a == 10'd0);\n  wire zero_b   = e_b_zero & (f_b == 10'd0);\n  wire nan_a    = e_a_ones & (f_a != 10'd0);\n  wire nan_b    = e_b_ones & (f_b != 10'd0);\n  wire inf_a    = e_a_ones & (f_a == 10'd0);\n  wire inf_b    = e_b_ones & (f_b == 10'd0);\n\n  wire nan_in  = nan_a | nan_b | (inf_a & zero_b) | (zero_a & inf_b);\n  wire inf_in  = inf_a | inf_b;\n  wire zero_in = zero_a | zero_b;\n  wire s_y     = s_a ^ s_b;\n\n  wire [10:0] m_a    = {~e_a_zero, f_a};\n  wire [10:0] m_b    = {~e_b_zero, f_b};\n  wire [4:0]  ea_eff = e_a_zero ? 5'd1 : e_a;\n  wire [4:0]  eb_eff = e_b_zero ? 5'd1 : e_b;\n\n  wire [PROD_W-1:0] prod = m_a * m_b;\n\n  // Leading-zero count of prod[21:10].  lz=0 -> bit21 is the leading one,\n  // lz=11 -> bit10, lz=12 -> no normal significand bit (subnormal path).\n  reg [4:0] lz;\n  always @* begin\n    casez (prod[21:10])\n      12'b1???????????: lz = 5'd0;\n      12'b01??????????: lz = 5'd1;\n      12'b001?????????: lz = 5'd2;\n      12'b0001????????: lz = 5'd3;\n      12'b00001???????: lz = 5'd4;\n      12'b000001??????: lz = 5'd5;\n      12'b0000001?????: lz = 5'd6;\n      12'b00000001????: lz = 5'd7;\n      12'b000000001???: lz = 5'd8;\n      12'b0000000001??: lz = 5'd9;\n      12'b00000000001?: lz = 5'd10;\n      12'b000000000001: lz = 5'd11;\n      default:          lz = 5'd12;\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  wire [PROD_W-1:0] below;\n  genvar k;\n  generate\n    for (k = 0; k < PROD_W; k = k + 1) begin : g_below\n      assign below[k] = |prod[k:0];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  function bit_at;\n    input [PROD_W-1:0] v;\n    input integer      idx;\n    begin\n      bit_at = (idx >= 0 && idx < PROD_W) ? v[idx] : 1'b0;\n    end\n  endfunction\n\n  wire              need_right = (lz <= 5'd11);\n  wire [4:0]        sr         = 5'd11 - lz;\n  wire [4:0]        sl         = lz - 5'd11;\n  wire [PROD_W-1:0] shifted    = need_right ? (prod >> sr) : (prod << sl);\n  wire [10:0]       mant_pre   = shifted[10:0];\n\n  wire guard    = need_right ? bit_at(prod,  sr - 1) : 1'b0;\n  wire sticky   = need_right ? bit_at(below, sr - 2) : 1'b0;\n  wire round_up = guard & (sticky | mant_pre[0]);\n\n  wire [11:0] mant_round = {1'b0, mant_pre} + {11'd0, round_up};\n  wire        carry      = mant_round[11];\n  wire [10:0] mant_post  = carry ? mant_round[11:1] : mant_round[10:0];\n  wire [9:0]  frac_norm  = mant_post[9:0];\n\n  wire [5:0] exp_sum     = {1'b0, ea_eff} + {1'b0, eb_eff};\n  wire [7:0] lhs         = {2'b0, exp_sum} + 8'd1 + {7'd0, carry};\n  wire [7:0] limit_under = BIAS + {3'd0, lz};\n  wire [7:0] limit_over  = BIAS + MAX_FINITE_E + {3'd0, lz};\n  wire       underflow   = (lhs <= limit_under);\n  wire       overflow    = (lhs >  limit_over);\n  wire [7:0] e_norm      = lhs - limit_under;\n  wire [4:0] exp_norm    = e_norm[4:0];\n\n  // subnormal result\n  integer           total_shift;\n  reg [PROD_W-1:0]  sub_shifted;\n  always @* begin\n    total_shift = (BIAS + 10 + 1) - $signed({1'b0, exp_sum});\n    sub_shifted = (total_shift >= 0 && total_shift < PROD_W) ? (prod >> total_shift) : {PROD_W{1'b0}};\n  end\n  wire [9:0]  frac_trunc = sub_shifted[9:0];\n  wire        guard_s    = bit_at(prod,  total_shift - 1);\n  wire        sticky_s   = bit_at(below, total_shift - 2);\n  wire        round_s    = guard_s & (sticky_s | frac_trunc[0]);\n  wire [10:0] frac_sum   = {1'b0, frac_trunc} + {10'd0, round_s};\n  wire        carry_s    = frac_sum[10];\n  wire [9:0]  frac_sub   = frac_sum[9:0];\n  wire [4:0]  exp_sub    = {4'd0, carry_s};\n\n  wire is_nan      = nan_in;\n  wire is_inf      = ~is_nan & (inf_in | (overflow & ~zero_in));\n  wire is_sub      = ~is_nan & ~is_inf & underflow;\n  wire sub_is_zero = is_sub & (exp_sub == 5'd0) & (frac_sub == 10'd0);\n  wire is_zero     = ~is_nan & ~is_inf & (zero_in | sub_is_zero);\n\n  wire [4:0] exp_y  = (is_nan | is_inf) ? 5'd31 : is_zero ? 5'd0 : is_sub ? exp_sub : exp_norm;\n  wire [9:0] frac_y = is_nan ? 10'h200 : (is_inf | is_zero) ? 10'd0 : is_sub ? frac_sub : frac_norm;\n  wire       sign_y = is_nan ? 1'b0 : s_y;\n\n  assign y = {sign_y, exp_y, frac_y};\nendmodule")

  -> create_file(filename='fp_mul_v2.v', content="// fp_mul_e5f10 -- v2: decomposed 11x11 significand product into a 10x10\n// multiply plus small adders.\nmodule fp_mul_e5f10 (a, b, y);\n  input  [15:0] a;\n  input  [15:0] b;\n  output [15:0] y;\n\n  localparam BIAS         = 15;\n  localparam MAX_E        = 31;\n  localparam MAX_FINITE_E = 30;\n  localparam PROD_W       = 22;\n\n  wire       s_a = a[15];\n  wire       s_b = b[15];\n  wire [4:0] e_a = a[14:10];\n  wire [4:0] e_b = b[14:10];\n  wire [9:0] f_a = a[9:0];\n  wire [9:0] f_b = b[9:0];\n\n  wire e_a_zero = (e_a == 5'd0);\n  wire e_b_zero = (e_b == 5'd0);\n  wire e_a_ones = (e_a == MAX_E);\n  wire e_b_ones = (e_b == MAX_E);\n  wire zero_a   = e_a_zero & (f_a == 10'd0);\n  wire zero_b   = e_b_zero & (f_b == 10'd0);\n  wire nan_a    = e_a_ones & (f_a != 10'd0);\n  wire nan_b    = e_b_ones & (f_b != 10'd0);\n  wire inf_a    = e_a_ones & (f_a == 10'd0);\n  wire inf_b    = e_b_ones & (f_b == 10'd0);\n\n  wire nan_in  = nan_a | nan_b | (inf_a & zero_b) | (zero_a & inf_b);\n  wire inf_in  = inf_a | inf_b;\n  wire zero_in = zero_a | zero_b;\n  wire s_y     = s_a ^ s_b;\n\n  // Decomposed significand product: m = hidden + fraction, hidden bit is 10.\n  // prod = f_a*f_b + h_a*f_b<<10 + h_b*f_a<<10 + h_a*h_b<<20\n  wire h_a = ~e_a_zero;\n  wire h_b = ~e_b_zero;\n\n  wire [19:0] ff = f_a * f_b;\n  wire [10:0] cross_sum;\n  assign cross_sum = {1'b0, (h_a ? f_b : 10'd0)} + {1'b0, (h_b ? f_a : 10'd0)};\n\n  wire [10:0] mid = ff[19:10] + {1'b0, cross_sum[9:0]};\n  wire [1:0]  upper = {1'b0, mid[10]} + {1'b0, cross_sum[10]} + {1'b0, (h_a & h_b)};\n\n  wire [PROD_W-1:0] prod = {upper, mid[9:0], ff[9:0]};\n\n  wire [4:0]  ea_eff = e_a_zero ? 5'd1 : e_a;\n  wire [4:0]  eb_eff = e_b_zero ? 5'd1 : e_b;\n\n  // Leading-zero count of prod[21:10].  lz=12 is only used as a subnormal\n  // sentinel (all normal-path significant bits are zero).\n  wire [4:0]  lz;\n  wire [11:0] top = prod[21:10];\n  assign lz = top[11] ? 5'd0  :\n              top[10] ? 5'd1  :\n              top[9]  ? 5'd2  :\n              top[8]  ? 5'd3  :\n              top[7]  ? 5'd4  :\n              top[6]  ? 5'd5  :\n              top[5]  ? 5'd6  :\n              top[4]  ? 5'd7  :\n              top[3]  ? 5'd8  :\n              top[2]  ? 5'd9  :\n              top[1]  ? 5'd10 :\n              top[0]  ? 5'd11 : 5'd12;\n\n  wire [PROD_W-1:0] below;\n  genvar k;\n  generate\n    for (k = 0; k < PROD_W; k = k + 1) begin : g_below\n      assign below[k] = |prod[k:0];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  function bit_at;\n    input [PROD_W-1:0] v;\n    input integer      idx;\n    begin\n      bit_at = (idx >= 0 && idx < PROD_W) ? v[idx] : 1'b0;\n    end\n  endfunction\n\n  wire              need_right = (lz <= 5'd11);\n  wire [4:0]        sr         = 5'd11 - lz;\n  wire [4:0]        sl         = lz - 5'd11;\n  wire [PROD_W-1:0] shifted    = need_right ? (prod >> sr) : (prod << sl);\n  wire [10:0]       mant_pre   = shifted[10:0];\n\n  wire guard    = need_right ? bit_at(prod,  sr - 1) : 1'b0;\n  wire sticky   = need_right ? bit_at(below, sr - 2) : 1'b0;\n  wire round_up = guard & (sticky | mant_pre[0]);\n\n  wire [11:0] mant_round = {1'b0, mant_pre} + {11'd0, round_up};\n  wire        carry      = mant_round[11];\n  wire [10:0] mant_post  = carry ? mant_round[11:1] : mant_round[10:0];\n  wire [9:0]  frac_norm  = mant_post[9:0];\n\n  wire [5:0] exp_sum     = {1'b0, ea_eff} + {1'b0, eb_eff};\n  wire [7:0] lhs         = {2'b0, exp_sum} + 8'd1 + {7'd0, carry};\n  wire [7:0] limit_under = BIAS + {3'd0, lz};\n  wire [7:0] limit_over  = BIAS + MAX_FINITE_E + {3'd0, lz};\n  wire       underflow   = (lhs <= limit_under);\n  wire       overflow    = (lhs >  limit_over);\n  wire [7:0] e_norm      = lhs - limit_under;\n  wire [4:0] exp_norm    = e_norm[4:0];\n\n  // subnormal result\n  integer           total_shift;\n  reg [PROD_W-1:0]  sub_shifted;\n  always @* begin\n    total_shift = (BIAS + 10 + 1) - $signed({1'b0, exp_sum});\n    sub_shifted = (total_shift >= 0 && total_shift < PROD_W) ? (prod >> total_shift) : {PROD_W{1'b0}};\n  end\n  wire [9:0]  frac_trunc = sub_shifted[9:0];\n  wire        guard_s    = bit_at(prod,  total_shift - 1);\n  wire        sticky_s   = bit_at(below, total_shift - 2);\n  wire        round_s    = guard_s & (sticky_s | frac_trunc[0]);\n  wire [10:0] frac_sum   = {1'b0, frac_trunc} + {10'd0, round_s};\n  wire        carry_s    = frac_sum[10];\n  wire [9:0]  frac_sub   = frac_sum[9:0];\n  wire [4:0]  exp_sub    = {4'd0, carry_s};\n\n  wire is_nan      = nan_in;\n  wire is_inf      = ~is_nan & (inf_in | (overflow & ~zero_in));\n  wire is_sub      = ~is_nan & ~is_inf & underflow;\n  wire sub_is_zero = is_sub & (exp_sub == 5'd0) & (frac_sub == 10'd0);\n  wire is_zero     = ~is_nan & ~is_inf & (zero_in | sub_is_zero);\n\n  wire [4:0] exp_y  = (is_nan | is_inf) ? 5'd31 : is_zero ? 5'd0 : is_sub ? exp_sub : exp_norm;\n  wire [9:0] frac_y = is_nan ? 10'h200 : (is_inf | is_zero) ? 10'd0 : is_sub ? frac_sub : frac_norm;\n  wire       sign_y = is_nan ? 1'b0 : s_y;\n\n  assign y = {sign_y, exp_y, frac_y};\nendmodule")

The normal result is then\n  // unused, and sr wraps harmlessly to 31.\n  wire [4:0]  lz;\n  wire [11:0] top = prod[21:10];\n  assign lz = top[11] ? 5'd0  :\n              top[10] ? 5'd1  :\n              top[9]  ? 5'd2  :\n              top[8]  ? 5'd3  :\n              top[7]  ? 5'd4  :\n              top[6]  ? 5'd5  :\n              top[5]  ? 5'd6  :\n              top[4]  ? 5'd7  :\n              top[3]  ? 5'd8  :\n              top[2]  ? 5'd9  :\n              top[1]  ? 5'd10 :\n              top[0]  ? 5'd11 : 5'd12;\n\n  wire [PROD_W-1:0] below;\n  genvar k;\n  generate\n    for (k = 0; k < PROD_W; k = k + 1) begin : g_below\n      assign below[k] = |prod[k:0];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  function bit_at;\n    input [PROD_W-1:0] v;\n    input integer      idx;\n    begin\n      bit_at = (idx >= 0 && idx < PROD_W) ? v[idx] : 1'b0;\n    end\n  endfunction\n\n  wire [4:0]        sr       = 5'd11 - lz;\n  wire [PROD_W-1:0] shifted  = prod >> sr;\n  wire [10:0]       mant_pre = shifted[10:0];\n\n  wire guard    = bit_at(prod,  sr - 1);\n  wire sticky   = bit_at(below, sr - 2);\n  wire round_up = guard & (sticky | mant_pre[0]);\n\n  wire [11:0] mant_round = {1'b0, mant_pre} + {11'd0, round_up};\n  wire        carry      = mant_round[11];\n  wire [10:0] mant_post  = carry ? mant_round[11:1] : mant_round[10:0];\n  wire [9:0]  frac_norm  = mant_post[9:0];\n\n  wire [5:0] exp_sum     = {1'b0, ea_eff} + {1'b0, eb_eff};\n  wire [7:0] lhs         = {2'b0, exp_sum} + 8'd1 + {7'd0, carry};\n  wire [7:0] limit_under = BIAS + {3'd0, lz};\n  wire [7:0] limit_over  = BIAS + MAX_FINITE_E + {3'd0, lz};\n  wire       underflow   = (lhs <= limit_under);\n  wire       overflow    = (lhs >  limit_over);\n  wire [7:0] e_norm      = lhs - limit_under;\n  wire [4:0] exp_norm    = e_norm[4:0];\n\n  // subnormal result\n  integer           total_shift;\n  reg [PROD_W-1:0]  sub_shifted;\n  always @* begin\n    total_shift = (BIAS + 10 + 1) - $signed({1'b0, exp_sum});\n    sub_shifted = (total_shift >= 0 && total_shift < PROD_W) ? (prod >> total_shift) : {PROD_W{1'b0}};\n  end\n  wire [9:0]  frac_trunc = sub_shifted[9:0];\n  wire        guard_s    = bit_at(prod,  total_shift - 1);\n  wire        sticky_s   = bit_at(below, total_shift - 2);\n  wire        round_s    = guard_s & (sticky_s | frac_trunc[0]);\n  wire [10:0] frac_sum   = {1'b0, frac_trunc} + {10'd0, round_s};\n  wire        carry_s    = frac_sum[10];\n  wire [9:0]  frac_sub   = frac_sum[9:0];\n  wire [4:0]  exp_sub    = {4'd0, carry_s};\n\n  wire is_nan      = nan_in;\n  wire is_inf      = ~is_nan & (inf_in | (overflow & ~zero_in));\n  wire is_sub      = ~is_nan & ~is_inf & underflow;\n  wire sub_is_zero = is_sub & (exp_sub == 5'd0) & (frac_sub == 10'd0);\n  wire is_zero     = ~is_nan & ~is_inf & (zero_in | sub_is_zero);\n\n  wire [4:0] exp_y  = (is_nan | is_inf) ? 5'd31 : is_zero ? 5'd0 : is_sub ? exp_sub : exp_norm;\n  wire [9:0] frac_y = is_nan ? 10'h200 : (is_inf | is_zero) ? 10'd0 : is_sub ? frac_sub : frac_norm;\n  wire       sign_y = is_nan ? 1'b0 : s_y;\n\n  assign y = {sign_y, exp_y, frac_y};\nendmodule")

[TOOL RESULT]
[Step 24/60]
  assign lz = top[11] ? 5'd0  :
              top[10] ? 5'd1  :
              top[9]  ? 5'd2  :
              top[8]  ? 5'd3  :
              top[7]  ? 5'd4  :
              top[6]  ? 5'd5  :
              top[5]  ? 5'd6  :
              top[4]  ? 5'd7  :
              top[3]  ? 5'd8  :
              top[2]  ? 5'd9  :
              top[1]  ? 5'd10 :
              top[0]  ? 5'd11 : 5'd12;

  wire [PROD_W-1:0] below;
  genvar k;
  generate
    for (k = 0; k < PROD_W; k = k + 1) begin : g_below
      assign below[k] = |prod[k:0];
    end
  endgenerate

  // Normal path: shift by sr-1, so shifted_n[0] is the guard bit and
  // shifted_n[11:1] is the 11-bit pre-round significand.
  wire [4:0] sr = 5'd11 - lz;
  wire [PROD_W-1:0] shifted_n;
  assign shifted_n = (sr == 5'd0) ? {10'd0, prod[10:0], 1'b0} : (prod >> (sr - 5'd1));

  wire [10:0] mant_pre = shifted_n[11:1];
  wire        guard    = shifted_n[0];
  wire        sticky   = (sr <= 5'd1) ? 1'b0 : below[sr - 5'd2];
  wire        round_up = guard & (sticky | mant_pre[0]);

  wire [11:0] mant_round = {1'b0, mant_pre} + {11'd0, round_up};
  wire        carry      = mant_round[11];
  wire [10:0] mant_post  = carry ? mant_round[11:1] : mant_round[10:0];
  wire [9:0]  frac_norm  = mant_post[9:0];

  wire [5:0] exp_sum     = {1'b0, ea_eff} + {1'b0, eb_eff};
  wire [7:0] lhs         = {2'b0, exp_sum} + 8'd1 + {7'd0, carry};
  wire [7:0] limit_under = BIAS + {3'd0, lz};
  wire [7:0] limit_over  = BIAS + MAX_FINITE_E + {3'd0, lz};
  wire       underflow   = (lhs <= limit_under);
  wire       overflow    = (lhs >  limit_over);
  wire [7:0] e_norm      = lhs - limit_under;
  wire [4:0] exp_norm    = e_norm[4:0];

  // Subnormal path: shift by total_shift-1, so shifted_s[0] is the guard bit
  // and shifted_s[10:1] is the truncated fraction.
  integer           total_shift;
  reg [PROD_W-1:0]  shifted_s;
  always @* begin
    total_shift = (BIAS + 10 + 1) - $signed({1'b0, exp_sum});
    if (total_shift >= 1 && total_shift < PROD_W + 1)
      shifted_s = prod >> (total_shift - 1);
    else
      shifted_s = {PROD_W{1'b0}};
  end

  wire [9:0]  frac_trunc = shifted_s[10:1];
  wire        guard_s    = shifted_s[0];
  wire        sticky_s   = (total_shift >= 2 && total_shift < PROD_W + 2) ? below[total_shift - 2] : 1'b0;
  wire        round_s    = guard_s & (sticky_s | frac_trunc[0]);
  wire [10:0] frac_sum   = {1'b0, frac_trunc} + {10'd0, round_s};
  wire        carry_s    = frac_sum[10];
  wire [9:0]  frac_sub   = frac_sum[9:0];
  wire [4:0]  exp_sub    = {4'd0, carry_s};

  wire is_nan      = nan_in;
  wire is_inf      = ~is_nan & (inf_in | (overflow & ~zero_in));
  wire is_sub      = ~is_nan & ~is_inf & underflow;
  wire sub_is_zero = is_sub & (exp_sub == 5'd0) & (frac_sub == 10'd0);
  wire is_zero     = ~is_nan & ~is_inf & (zero_in | sub_is_zero);

  wire [4:0] exp_y  = (is_nan | is_inf) ? 5'd31 : is_zero ? 5'd0 : is_sub ? exp_sub : exp_norm;
  wire [9:0] frac_y = is_nan ? 10'h200 : (is_inf | is_zero) ? 10'd0 : is_sub ? frac_sub : frac_norm;
  wire       sign_y = is_nan ? 1'b0 : s_y;

  assign y = {sign_y, exp_y, frac_y};
endmodule

[TOOL RESULT]
[Step 25/60]
  wire [PROD_W-1:0] prod = m_a * m_b;

  // Leading-zero count of prod[21:10].  lz=0 -> bit21 is the leading one,
  // lz=11 -> bit10, lz=12 -> no normal significand bit (subnormal path).
  wire [4:0] lz;
  wire [11:0] top = prod[21:10];
  assign lz = top[11] ? 5'd0  :
              top[10] ? 5'd1  :
              top[9]  ? 5'd2  :
              top[8]  ? 5'd3  :
              top[7]  ? 5'd4  :
              top[6]  ? 5'd5  :
              top[5]  ? 5'd6  :
              top[4]  ? 5'd7  :
              top[3]  ? 5'd8  :
              top[2]  ? 5'd9  :
              top[1]  ? 5'd10 :
              top[0]  ? 5'd11 : 5'd12;

  wire [PROD_W-1:0] below;
  genvar k;
  generate
    for (k = 0; k < PROD_W; k = k + 1) begin : g_below
      assign below[k] = |prod[k:0];
    end
  endgenerate

  function bit_at;
    input [PROD_W-1:0] v;
    input integer      idx;
    begin
      bit_at = (idx >= 0 && idx < PROD_W) ? v[idx] : 1'b0;
    end
  endfunction

  wire              need_right = (lz <= 5'd11);
  wire [4:0]        sr         = 5'd11 - lz;
  wire [4:0]        sl         = lz - 5'd11;
  wire [PROD_W-1:0] shifted    = need_right ? (prod >> sr) : (prod << sl);
  wire [10:0]       mant_pre   = shifted[10:0];

  wire guard    = need_right ? bit_at(prod,  sr - 1) : 1'b0;
  wire sticky   = need_right ? bit_at(below, sr - 2) : 1'b0;
  wire round_up = guard & (sticky | mant_pre[0]);

  wire [11:0] mant_round = {1'b0, mant_pre} + {11'd0, round_up};
  wire        carry      = mant_round[11];
  wire [10:0] mant_post  = carry ? mant_round[11:1] : mant_round[10:0];
  wire [9:0]  frac_norm  = mant_post[9:0];

  wire [5:0] exp_sum     = {1'b0, ea_eff} + {1'b0, eb_eff};
  wire [7:0] lhs         = {2'b0, exp_sum} + 8'd1 + {7'd0, carry};
  wire [7:0] limit_under = BIAS + {3'd0, lz};
  wire [7:0] limit_over  = BIAS + MAX_FINITE_E + {3'd0, lz};
  wire       underflow   = (lhs <= limit_under);
  wire       overflow    = (lhs >  limit_over);
  wire [7:0] e_norm      = lhs - limit_under;
  wire [4:0] exp_norm    = e_norm[4:0];

  // subnormal result
  integer           total_shift;
  reg [PROD_W-1:0]  sub_shifted;
  always @* begin
    total_shift = (BIAS + 10 + 1) - $signed({1'b0, exp_sum});
    sub_shifted = (total_shift >= 0 && total_shift < PROD_W) ? (prod >> total_shift) : {PROD_W{1'b0}};
  end
  wire [9:0]  frac_trunc = sub_shifted[9:0];
  wire        guard_s    = bit_at(prod,  total_shift - 1);
  wire        sticky_s   = bit_at(below, total_shift - 2);
  wire        round_s    = guard_s & (sticky_s | frac_trunc[0]);
  wire [10:0] frac_sum   = {1'b0, frac_trunc} + {10'd0, round_s};
  wire        carry_s    = frac_sum[10];
  wire [9:0]  frac_sub   = frac_sum[9:0];
  wire [4:0]  exp_sub    = {4'd0, carry_s};

  wire is_nan      = nan_in;
  wire is_inf      = ~is_nan & (inf_in | (overflow & ~zero_in));
  wire is_sub      = ~is_nan & ~is_inf & underflow;
  wire sub_is_zero = is_sub & (exp_sub == 5'd0) & (frac_sub == 10'd0);
  wire is_zero     = ~is_nan & ~is_inf & (zero_in | sub_is_zero);

  wire [4:0] exp_y  = (is_nan | is_inf) ? 5'd31 : is_zero ? 5'd0 : is_sub ? exp_sub : exp_norm;
  wire [9:0] frac_y = is_nan ? 10'h200 : (is_inf | is_zero) ? 10'd0 : is_sub ? frac_sub : frac_norm;
  wire       sign_y = is_nan ? 1'b0 : s_y;

  assign y = {sign_y, exp_y, frac_y};
endmodule

5'd1 : e_a;\n  wire [4:0]  eb_eff = e_b_zero ? 5'd1 : e_b;\n\n  wire [PROD_W-1:0] prod = m_a * m_b;\n\n  wire [4:0]  lz;\n  wire [11:0] top = prod[21:10];\n  assign lz = top[11] ? 5'd0  :\n              top[10] ? 5'd1  :\n              top[9]  ? 5'd2  :\n              top[8]  ? 5'd3  :\n              top[7]  ? 5'd4  :\n              top[6]  ? 5'd5  :\n              top[5]  ? 5'd6  :\n              top[4]  ? 5'd7  :\n              top[3]  ? 5'd8  :\n              top[2]  ? 5'd9  :\n              top[1]  ? 5'd10 :\n              top[0]  ? 5'd11 : 5'd12;\n\n  wire [PROD_W-1:0] below;\n  genvar k;\n  generate\n    for (k = 0; k < PROD_W; k = k + 1) begin : g_below\n      assign below[k] = |prod[k:0];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  wire [5:0] exp_sum     = {1'b0, ea_eff} + {1'b0, eb_eff};\n  wire [6:0] ts_s        = 7'd26 - {1'b0, exp_sum};\n  wire [4:0] sr          = 5'd11 - lz;\n  wire [7:0] lhs         = {2'b0, exp_sum} + 8'd1;  // carry added later\n  wire [7:0] limit_under = BIAS + {3'd0, lz};\n  wire [7:0] limit_over  = BIAS + MAX_FINITE_E + {3'd0, lz};\n  wire       underflow   = ((lhs + {7'd0, carry}) <= limit_under); // placeholder removed below\n  wire       overflow    = ((lhs + {7'd0, carry}) >  limit_over);\n\n  // Shared shifter:\n  //   subnormal path: shifted[0]=guard, shifted[10:1]=fraction\n  //   normal path:    shifted[0]=guard, shifted[11:1]=pre-round significand\n  wire        use_sub    = underflow;\n  wire        special_n  = ~use_sub & (sr == 5'd0);\n  wire        special_s  =  use_sub & (ts_s == 7'd0);\n  wire        special    = special_n | special_s;\n  wire [4:0]  sr_minus1  = sr - 5'd1;\n  wire [4:0]  ts_minus1  = ts_s[4:0] - 5'd1;\n  wire [4:0]  raw_shift  = use_sub ? ts_minus1 : sr_minus1;\n  wire [PROD_W-1:0] special_val = special_n ? {10'd0, prod[10:0], 1'b0} :\n                                             {10'd0, prod[9:0], 1'b0};\n  wire [PROD_W-1:0] shifted   = special ? special_val : (prod >> raw_shift);\n\n  wire [10:0] mant_pre = shifted[11:1];\n  wire        guard    = shifted[0];\n  wire        sticky   = use_sub ? 1'b0 :\n                        (sr <= 5'd1) ? 1'b0 : below[sr - 5'd2];\n  wire        round_up = guard & (sticky | mant_pre[0]);\n\n  wire [11:0] mant_round = {1'b0, mant_pre} + {11'd0, round_up};\n  wire        carry      = mant_round[11];\n  wire [10:0] mant_post  = carry ? mant_round[11:1] : mant_round[10:0];\n  wire [9:0]  frac_norm  = mant_post[9:0];\n\n  // Rebuild underflow/overflow including the rounding carry.\n  // (The earlier use_sub is only a shift select and is safe: rounding carry\n  // can turn a subnormal into the smallest normal for the sub path.)\n  assign lhs_plus = {2'b0, exp_sum} + 8'd1 + {7'd0, carry};\n  assign underflow = (lhs_plus <= limit_under);\n  assign overflow  = (lhs_plus >  limit_over);\n  wire [7:0] e_norm   = lhs_plus - limit_under;\n  wire [4:0] exp_norm = e_norm[4:0];\n\n  wire [9:0]  frac_trunc = shifted[10:1];\n  wire        guard_s    = shifted[0];\n  wire        sticky_s   = (ts_s >= 7'd2 && ts_s < 7'd23) ? below[ts_s[4:0] - 5'd2] : 1'b0;\n  wire        round_s    = guard_s & (sticky_s | frac_trunc[0]);\n  wire [10:0] frac_sum   = {1'b0, frac_trunc} + {10'd0, round_s};\n  wire        carry_s    = frac_sum[10];\n  wire [9:0]  frac_sub   = frac_sum[9:0];\n  wire [4:0]  exp_sub    = {4'd0, carry_s};\n\n  wire is_nan      = nan_in;\n  wire is_inf      = ~is_nan & (inf_in | (overflow & ~zero_in));\n  wire is_sub      = ~is_nan & ~is_inf & underflow;\n  wire sub_is_zero = is_sub & (exp_sub == 5'd0) & (frac_sub == 10'd0);\n  wire is_zero     = ~is_nan & ~is_inf & (zero_in | sub_is_zero);\n\n  wire [4:0] exp_y  = (is_nan | is_inf) ? (prod >> ts_m1) : 11'd0;\n\n  wire [9:0]  frac_trunc = shifted_s[10:1];\n  wire        guard_s    = shifted_s[0];\n  wire        sticky_s   = (total_shift >= 7'd2 && total_shift < 7'd23) ? below[total_shift[4:0] - 5'd2] : 1'b0;\n  wire        round_s    = guard_s & (sticky_s | frac_trunc[0]);\n  wire [9:0]  frac_sub_inc = frac_trunc + {9'd0, round_s};\n  wire        carry_s    = round_s & (&frac_trunc);\n  wire [9:0]  frac_sub   = carry_s ? 10'd0 : frac_sub_inc;\n\n  wire is_nan      = nan_in;\n  wire is_inf      = ~is_nan & (inf_in | (overflow & ~zero_in));\n  wire is_sub      = ~is_nan & ~is_inf & underflow;\n  wire sub_is_zero = is_sub & ~carry_s & (frac_sub_inc == 10'd0);\n  wire is_zero     = ~is_nan & ~is_inf & (zero_in | sub_is_zero);\n\n  wire [4:0] exp_y  = (is_nan | is_inf) ? 5'd31 : is_zero ? 5'd0 : is_sub ? {4'd0, carry_s} : exp_norm;\n  wire [9:0] frac_y = is_nan ? 10'h200 : (is_inf | is_zero) ? 10'd0 : is_sub ? frac_sub : frac_norm;\n  wire       sign_y = is_nan ? 1'b0 : s_y;\n\n  assign y = {sign_y, exp_y, frac_y};\nendmodule")

  -> create_file(filename='fp_mul_v11.v', content="// fp_mul_e5f10 -- v11: v8 with only the normal shifter truncated to its\n// 12 useful output bits.\nmodule fp_mul_e5f10 (a, b, y);\n  input  [15:0] a;\n  input  [15:0] b;\n  output [15:0] y;\n\n  localparam BIAS         = 15;\n  localparam MAX_E        = 31;\n  localparam PROD_W       = 22;\n\n  wire       s_a = a[15];\n  wire       s_b = b[15];\n  wire [4:0] e_a = a[14:10];\n  wire [4:0] e_b = b[14:10];\n  wire [9:0] f_a = a[9:0];\n  wire [9:0] f_b = b[9:0];\n\n  wire e_a_zero = (e_a == 5'd0);\n  wire e_b_zero = (e_b == 5'd0);\n  wire e_a_ones = (e_a == MAX_E);\n  wire e_b_ones = (e_b == MAX_E);\n  wire zero_a   = e_a_zero & (f_a == 10'd0);\n  wire zero_b   = e_b_zero & (f_b == 10'd0);\n  wire nan_a    = e_a_ones & (f_a != 10'd0);\n  wire nan_b    = e_b_ones & (f_b != 10'd0);\n  wire inf_a    = e_a_ones & (f_a == 10'd0);\n  wire inf_b    = e_b_ones & (f_b == 10'd0);\n\n  wire nan_in  = nan_a | nan_b | (inf_a & zero_b) | (zero_a & inf_b);\n  wire inf_in  = inf_a | inf_b;\n  wire zero_in = zero_a | zero_b;\n  wire s_y     = s_a ^ s_b;\n\n  wire [10:0] m_a    = {~e_a_zero, f_a};\n  wire [10:0] m_b    = {~e_b_zero, f_b};\n  wire [4:0]  ea_eff = e_a_zero ? 5'd1 : e_a;\n  wire [4:0]  eb_eff = e_b_zero ? 5'd1 : e_b;\n\n  wire [PROD_W-1:0] prod = m_a * m_b;\n\n  wire [4:0]  lz;\n  wire [11:0] top = prod[21:10];\n  assign lz = top[11] ? 5'd0  :\n              top[10] ? 5'd1  :\n              top[9]  ? 5'd2  :\n              top[8]  ? 5'd3  :\n              top[7]  ? 5'd4  :\n              top[6]  ? 5'd5  :\n              top[5]  ? 5'd6  :\n              top[4]  ? 5'd7  :\n              top[3]  ? 5'd8  :\n              top[2]  ? 5'd9  :\n              top[1]  ? 5'd10 :\n              top[0]  ? 5'd11 : 5'd12;\n\n  wire [PROD_W-1:0] below;\n  genvar k;\n  generate\n    for (k = 0; k < PROD_W; k = k + 1) begin : g_below\n      assign below[k] = |prod[k:0];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  wire [5:0] exp_sum = {1'b0, ea_eff} + {1'b0, eb_eff};\n\n  // Normal path: shift by sr-1, so shifted_n[0] is the guard bit and\n  // shifted_n[11:1] is the 11-bit pre-round significand.\n  wire [4:0] sr = 5'd11 - lz;\n  wire [11:0] shifted_n;\n  assign shifted_n = (sr == 5'd0) ? {prod[10:0], 1'b0} :\n                                    (prod >> (sr - 5'd1));\n\n  wire [10:0] mant_pre = shifted_n[11:1];\n  wire        guard    = shifted_n[0];\n  wire        sticky   = (sr <= 5'd1) ? 1'b0 : below[sr - 5'd2];\n  wire        round_up = guard & (sticky | mant_pre[0]);\n\n  wire [9:0] frac_inc  = mant_pre[9:0] + {9'd0, round_up};\n  wire       carry     = round_up & (&mant_pre[9:0]);\n  wire [9:0] frac_norm = carry ? 10'd0 : frac_inc;\n\n  // exp_c = exp_sum + carry; normal unbiased exponent is exp_c-(14+lz).\n  wire [5:0] exp_c        = exp_sum + {5'd0, carry};\n  wire [5:0] limit_under  = 6'd14 + {1'b0, lz};\n  wire [5:0] limit_over   = 6'd44 + {1'b0, lz};\n  wire       underflow    = (exp_c <= limit_under);\n  wire       overflow     = (exp_c >  limit_over);\n  wire [5:0] e_norm       = exp_c - limit_under;\n  wire [4:0] exp_norm     = e_norm[4:0];\n\n  // Subnormal path: total_shift = 26 - exp_sum.\n  wire [6:0] total_shift = 7'd26 - {1'b0, exp_sum};\n  wire       ts_valid    = ({1'b0, exp_sum} <= 7'd25);\n  wire [4:0] ts_m1       = total_shift[4:0] - 5'd1;\n  wire [PROD_W-1:0] shifted_s = ts_valid ? (prod >> ts_m1) : {PROD_W{1'b0}};\n\n  wire [9:0]  frac_trunc = shifted_s[10:1];\n  wire        guard_s    = shifted_s[0];\n  wire        sticky_s   = (total_shift >= 7'd2 && total_shift < 7'd23) ? below[total_shift[4:0] - 5'd2] : 1'b0;\n  wire        round_s    = guard_s & (sticky_s | frac_trunc[0]);\n  wire [9:0]  frac_sub_inc = frac_trunc + {9'd0, round_s};\n  wire        carry_s    = round_s & (&frac_trunc);\n  wire [9:0]  frac_sub   = carry_s ? 10'd0 : frac_sub_inc;\n\n  wire is_nan      = nan_in;\n  wire is_inf      = ~is_nan & (inf_in | (overflow & ~zero_in));\n  wire is_sub      = ~is_nan & ~is_inf & underflow;\n  wire sub_is_zero = is_sub & ~carry_s & (frac_sub_inc == 10'd0);\n  wire is_zero     = ~is_nan & ~is_inf & (zero_in | sub_is_zero);\n\n  wire [4:0] exp_y  = (is_nan | is_inf) ? 5'd31 : is_zero ? 5'd0 : is_sub ? {4'd0, carry_s} : exp_norm;\n  wire [9:0] frac_y = is_nan ? 10'h200 : (is_inf | is_zero) ? 10'd0 : is_sub ? frac_sub : frac_norm;\n  wire       sign_y = is_nan ? 1'b0 : s_y;\n\n  assign y = {sign_y, exp_y, frac_y};\nendmodule")

5'd1 : e_a;\n  wire [4:0]  eb_eff = e_b_zero ? 5'd1 : e_b;\n\n  wire [PROD_W-1:0] prod = m_a * m_b;\n\n  wire [4:0]  lz;\n  wire [11:0] top = prod[21:10];\n  assign lz = top[11] ? 5'd0  :\n              top[10] ? 5'd1  :\n              top[9]  ? 5'd2  :\n              top[8]  ? 5'd3  :\n              top[7]  ? 5'd4  :\n              top[6]  ? 5'd5  :\n              top[5]  ? 5'd6  :\n              top[4]  ? 5'd7  :\n              top[3]  ? 5'd8  :\n              top[2]  ? 5'd9  :\n              top[1]  ? 5'd10 :\n              top[0]  ? 5'd11 : 5'd12;\n\n  wire [PROD_W-1:0] below;\n  genvar k;\n  generate\n    for (k = 0; k < PROD_W; k = k + 1) begin : g_below\n      assign below[k] = |prod[k:0];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  wire [5:0] exp_sum = {1'b0, ea_eff} + {1'b0, eb_eff};\n\n  // Normal path: shift by sr-1, so shifted_n[0] is the guard bit and\n  // shifted_n[11:1] is the 11-bit pre-round significand.\n  wire [4:0] sr = 5'd11 - lz;\n  wire [PROD_W-1:0] shifted_n;\n  assign shifted_n = (sr == 5'd0) ? {10'd0, prod[10:0], 1'b0} : (prod >> (sr - 5'd1));\n\n  wire [10:0] mant_pre = shifted_n[11:1];\n  wire        guard    = shifted_n[0];\n  wire        sticky   = (sr <= 5'd1) ? 1'b0 : below[sr - 5'd2];\n  wire        round_up = guard & (sticky | mant_pre[0]);\n\n  wire [9:0] all_sig = mant_pre[9:0];\n  wire [9:0] frac_inc  = all_sig + {9'd0, round_up};\n  wire       carry     = round_up & (&all_sig);\n  wire [9:0] frac_norm = carry ? 10'd0 : frac_inc;\n\n  // exp_c = exp_sum + carry; normal unbiased exponent is exp_c-(14+lz).\n  wire [5:0] exp_c        = exp_sum + {5'd0, carry};\n  wire [5:0] limit_under  = 6'd14 + {1'b0, lz};\n  wire [5:0] limit_over   = 6'd44 + {1'b0, lz};\n  wire       underflow    = (exp_c <= limit_under);\n  wire       overflow     = (exp_c >  limit_over);\n  wire [5:0] e_norm       = exp_c - limit_under;\n  wire [4:0] exp_norm     = e_norm[4:0];\n\n  // Subnormal path: total_shift = 26 - exp_sum.\n  wire [6:0] total_shift = 7'd26 - {1'b0, exp_sum};\n  wire       ts_valid    = ({1'b0, exp_sum} <= 7'd25);\n  wire [4:0] ts_m1       = total_shift[4:0] - 5'd1;\n  wire [PROD_W-1:0] shifted_s = ts_valid ? (prod >> ts_m1) : {PROD_W{1'b0}};\n\n  wire [9:0]  frac_trunc = shifted_s[10:1];\n  wire        guard_s    = shifted_s[0];\n  wire        sticky_s   = (total_shift >= 7'd2 && total_shift < 7'd23) ? below[total_shift[4:0] - 5'd2] : 1'b0;\n  wire        round_s    = guard_s & (sticky_s | frac_trunc[0]);\n  wire [9:0]  frac_sub_inc = frac_trunc + {9'd0, round_s};\n  wire        carry_s    = round_s & (&frac_trunc);\n  wire [9:0]  frac_sub   = carry_s ? 10'd0 : frac_sub_inc;\n\n  wire is_nan      = nan_in;\n  wire is_inf      = ~is_nan & (inf_in | (overflow & ~zero_in));\n  wire is_sub      = ~is_nan & ~is_inf & underflow;\n  wire sub_is_zero = is_sub & ~carry_s & (frac_sub_inc == 10'd0);\n  wire is_zero     = ~is_nan & ~is_inf & (zero_in | sub_is_zero);\n\n  wire [4:0] exp_y  = (is_nan | is_inf) ? 5'd31 : is_zero ? 5'd0 : is_sub ? {4'd0, carry_s} : exp_norm;\n  wire [9:0] frac_y = is_nan ? 10'h200 : (is_inf | is_zero) ? 10'd0 : is_sub ? frac_sub : frac_norm;\n  wire       sign_y = is_nan ? 1'b0 : s_y;\n\n  assign y = {sign_y, exp_y, frac_y};\nendmodule")

[Step 46/60]
`timescale 1ns/10ps
// exhaustive-directed tb (fpmul_run)
// Golden-referenced directed testbench: compares the DUT against the embedded
// golden reference (fp_mul_e5f10_ref, generated from context/starting_point.py)
// over ~18.9M enumerated vectors — no stimuli file. Structure:
//   1. the 3 known subnormal-boundary regression pairs (+ golden self-checks)
//   2. every subnormal/zero a (2^11 sign+mantissa patterns) x 4096 structured b
//   3. 4096 structured a x every subnormal/zero b
//   4. 2^21 LFSR-driven broad pairs
// Structured operands: both signs x all 32 exponents x 64 mantissas
// (8 corners + 56 LFSR-derived). The retired file-driven testbench is kept at
// tb_vectors.sv (+ vectors.dat) for cross-checking; it is not compiled.
module tb;
  logic [15:0] a, b, y, y_ref;
  int errors;
  longint total;

  fp_mul_e5f10 dut (.a(a), .b(b), .y(y));
  fp_mul_e5f10_ref refm (.a(a), .b(b), .y(y_ref));

  function automatic [15:0] lfsr_next(input [15:0] s);
    lfsr_next = (s >> 1) ^ (s[0] ? 16'hB400 : 16'h0000);
  endfunction

  logic [15:0] structured [0:4095];
  localparam logic [9:0] MANT_CORNERS [0:7] =
      '{10'h000, 10'h001, 10'h002, 10'h003, 10'h3FF, 10'h3FE, 10'h2AA, 10'h155};

  task automatic check();
    #1;
    total = total + 1;
    if (y !== y_ref) begin
      if (errors < 10)
        $display("TB_ERROR a=%0d b=%0d expected_y=%0d actual_y=%0d", a, b, y_ref, y);
      errors = errors + 1;
    end
  endtask

  logic [15:0] lfsr, ra, rb;
  logic [9:0] mant;
  int idx;

  initial begin
    errors = 0; total = 0;

    lfsr = 16'hACE1;
    idx = 0;
    for (int s = 0; s < 2; s++)
      for (int e = 0; e < 32; e++)
        for (int m = 0; m < 64; m++) begin
          if (m < 8) mant = MANT_CORNERS[m];
          else begin lfsr = lfsr_next(lfsr); mant = lfsr[9:0]; end
          structured[idx] = {s[0], e[4:0], mant};
          idx = idx + 1;
        end

    a = 16'h0003; b = 16'h6801; check();
    if (y_ref !== 16'h0E02) begin errors++; $display("TB_ERROR golden self-check 1"); end
    a = 16'h8003; b = 16'h6479; check();
    if (y_ref !== 16'h8AB6) begin errors++; $display("TB_ERROR golden self-check 2"); end
    a = 16'h7521; b = 16'h0003; check();
    if (y_ref !== 16'h1BB2) begin errors++; $display("TB_ERROR golden self-check 3"); end

    for (int sa = 0; sa < 2048; sa++) begin
      a = {sa[10], 5'b00000, sa[9:0]};
      for (int i = 0; i < 4096; i++) begin b = structured[i]; check(); end
    end
    for (int i = 0; i < 4096; i++) begin
      a = structured[i];
      for (int sb = 0; sb < 2048; sb++) begin b = {sb[10], 5'b00000, sb[9:0]}; check(); end
    end
    ra = 16'h0001; rb = 16'hBEEF;
    for (int k = 0; k < (1 << 21); k++) begin
      ra = lfsr_next(ra); rb = lfsr_next(lfsr_next(rb));
      a = ra; b = rb; check();
    end

    $display("TB_SUMMARY total=%0d errors=%0d", total, errors);
    if (errors != 0) $fatal(1, "FAIL");
    $display("PASS");
    $finish;
  end
endmodule

[Step 48/60]
// fp_mul_e5f10 -- v8: v6 with manual rounding incrementers.
module fp_mul_e5f10 (a, b, y);
  input  [15:0] a;
  input  [15:0] b;
  output [15:0] y;

  localparam BIAS         = 15;
  localparam MAX_E        = 31;
  localparam MAX_FINITE_E = 30;
  localparam PROD_W       = 22;

  wire       s_a = a[15];
  wire       s_b = b[15];
  wire [4:0] e_a = a[14:10];
  wire [4:0] e_b = b[14:10];
  wire [9:0] f_a = a[9:0];
  wire [9:0] f_b = b[9:0];

  wire e_a_zero = (e_a == 5'd0);
  wire e_b_zero = (e_b == 5'd0);
  wire e_a_ones = (e_a == MAX_E);
  wire e_b_ones = (e_b == MAX_E);
  wire zero_a   = e_a_zero & (f_a == 10'd0);
  wire zero_b   = e_b_zero & (f_b == 10'd0);
  wire nan_a    = e_a_ones & (f_a != 10'd0);
  wire nan_b    = e_b_ones & (f_b != 10'd0);
  wire inf_a    = e_a_ones & (f_a == 10'd0);
  wire inf_b    = e_b_ones & (f_b == 10'd0);

  wire nan_in  = nan_a | nan_b | (inf_a & zero_b) | (zero_a & inf_b);
  wire inf_in  = inf_a | inf_b;
  wire zero_in = zero_a | zero_b;
  wire s_y     = s_a ^ s_b;

  wire [10:0] m_a    = {~e_a_zero, f_a};
  wire [10:0] m_b    = {~e_b_zero, f_b};
  wire [4:0]  ea_eff = e_a_zero ? 5'd1 : e_a;
  wire [4:0]  eb_eff = e_b_zero ? 5'd1 : e_b;

  wire [PROD_W-1:0] prod = m_a * m_b;

  wire [4:0]  lz;
  wire [11:0] top = prod[21:10];
  assign lz = top[11] ? 5'd0  :
              top[10] ? 5'd1  :
              top[9]  ? 5'd2  :
              top[8]  ? 5'd3  :
              top[7]  ? 5'd4  :
              top[6]  ? 5'd5  :
              top[5]  ? 5'd6  :
              top[4]  ? 5'd7  :
              top[3]  ? 5'd8  :
              top[2]  ? 5'd9  :
              top[1]  ? 5'd10 :
              top[0]  ? 5'd11 : 5'd12;

  wire [PROD_W-1:0] below;
  genvar k;
  generate
    for (k = 0; k < PROD_W; k = k + 1) begin : g_below
      assign below[k] = |prod[k:0];
    end
  endgenerate

  wire [5:0] exp_sum = {1'b0, ea_eff} + {1'b0, eb_eff};

  // Normal path: shift by sr-1, so shifted_n[0] is the guard bit and
  // shifted_n[11:1] is the 11-bit pre-round significand.
  wire [4:0] sr = 5'd11 - lz;
  wire [PROD_W-1:0] shifted_n;
  assign shifted_n = (sr == 5'd0) ? {10'd0, prod[10:0], 1'b0} : (prod >> (sr - 5'd1));

  wire [10:0] mant_pre = shifted_n[11:1];
  wire        guard    = shifted_n[0];
  wire        sticky   = (sr <= 5'd1) ? 1'b0 : below[sr - 5'd2];
  wire        round_up = guard & (sticky | mant_pre[0]);

  wire [9:0] frac_inc  = mant_pre[9:0] + {9'd0, round_up};
  wire       carry     = round_up & (&mant_pre);
  wire [9:0] frac_norm = carry ? 10'd0 : frac_inc;

  // exp_c = exp_sum + carry; normal unbiased exponent is
  // exp_c - (14 + lz).  underflow/overflow compares use 6-bit quantities.
  wire [5:0] exp_c     = exp_sum + {5'd0, carry};
  wire [5:0] limit_under = 6'd14 + {1'b0, lz};
  wire [5:0] limit_over  = 6'd44 + {1'b0, lz};
  wire       underflow   = (exp_c <= limit_under);
  wire       overflow    = (exp_c >  limit_over);
  wire [5:0] e_norm      = exp_c - limit_under;
  wire [4:0] exp_norm    = e_norm[4:0];

  // Subnormal path: total_shift = 26 - exp_sum.
  wire [7:0] exp_sum_biased = {2'b0, exp_sum} + 8'd15;
  wire [7:0] total_shift = 8'd41 - exp_sum_biased;
  wire       ts_valid    = (exp_sum_biased <= 8'd40);
  wire [4:0] ts_m1       = total_shift[4:0] - 5'd1;
  wire [PROD_W-1:0] shifted_s = ts_valid ? (prod >> ts_m1) : {PROD_W{1'b0}};

  wire [9:0]  frac_trunc = shifted_s[10:1];
  wire        guard_s    = shifted_s[0];
  wire        sticky_s   = (total_shift >= 8'd2 && total_shift < 8'd23) ? below[total_shift[4:0] - 5'd2] : 1'b0;
  wire        round_s    = guard_s & (sticky_s | frac_trunc[0]);
  wire [9:0]  frac_sub_inc = frac_trunc + {9'd0, round_s};
  wire        carry_s    = round_s & (&frac_trunc);
  wire [9:0]  frac_sub   = carry_s ? 10'd0 : frac_sub_inc;

  wire is_nan      = nan_in;
  wire is_inf      = ~is_nan & (inf_in | (overflow & ~zero_in));
  wire is_sub      = ~is_nan & ~is_inf & underflow;
  wire sub_is_zero = is_sub & ~carry_s & (frac_sub_inc == 10'd0);
  wire is_zero     = ~is_nan & ~is_inf & (zero_in | sub_is_zero);

  wire [4:0] exp_y  = (is_nan | is_inf) ? 5'd31 : is_zero ? 5'd0 : is_sub ? {4'd0, carry_s} : exp_norm;
  wire [9:0] frac_y = is_nan ? 10'h200 : (is_inf | is_zero) ? 10'd0 : is_sub ? frac_sub : frac_norm;
  wire       sign_y = is_nan ? 1'b0 : s_y;

  assign y = {sign_y, exp_y, frac_y};
endmodule

[ASSISTANT]
  -> edit_file(filename='fp_mul_v8.v', old_string="// Subnormal path: total_shift = 26 - exp_sum.\n  wire [7:0] exp_sum_biased = {2'b0, exp_sum} + 8'd15;\n  wire [7:0] total_shift = 8'd41 - exp_sum_biased;\n  wire       ts_valid    = (exp_sum_biased <= 8'd40);\n  wire [4:0] ts_m1       = total_shift[4:0] - 5'd1;\n  wire [PROD_W-1:0] shifted_s = ts_valid ? (prod >> ts_m1) : {PROD_W{1'b0}};\n\n  wire [9:0]  frac_trunc = shifted_s[10:1];\n  wire        guard_s    = shifted_s[0];\n  wire        sticky_s   = (total_shift >= 8'd2 && total_shift < 8'd23) ? below[total_shift[4:0] - 5'd2] : 1'b0;", new_string="// Subnormal path: total_shift = 26 - exp_sum.\n  wire [6:0] total_shift = 7'd26 - {1'b0, exp_sum};\n  wire       ts_valid    = ({1'b0, exp_sum} <= 7'd25);\n  wire [4:0] ts_m1       = total_shift[4:0] - 5'd1;\n  wire [PROD_W-1:0] shifted_s = ts_valid ? (prod >> ts_m1) : {PROD_W{1'b0}};\n\n  wire [9:0]  frac_trunc = shifted_s[10:1];\n  wire        guard_s    = shifted_s[0];\n  wire        sticky_s   = (total_shift >= 7'd2 && total_shift < 7'd23) ? below[total_shift[4:0] - 5'd2] : 1'b0;")

The rounding carry can\n  // only turn an underflowing case into the smallest normal, so the selected\n  // subnormal result (with its own tiny-integer exponent) remains correct.\n  wire [5:0] limit_under = 6'd14 + {1'b0, lz};\n  wire       use_sub_pre = (exp_sum <= limit_under);\n\n  wire [4:0] sr = 5'd11 - lz;\n  wire [6:0] total_shift = 7'd26 - {1'b0, exp_sum};\n\n  wire [4:0] normal_sh  = sr - 5'd1;     // sr=0 wraps to 31, corrected below\n  wire [4:0] sub_sh     = total_shift[4:0] - 5'd1;\n  wire [4:0] bar_sh     = use_sub_pre ? 10'd0 : frac_inc;\n\n  wire [5:0] exp_c        = exp_sum + {5'd0, carry};\n  wire [5:0] limit_over   = 6'd44 + {1'b0, lz};\n  wire       underflow    = (exp_c <= limit_under);\n  wire       overflow     = (exp_c >  limit_over);\n  wire [5:0] e_norm       = exp_c - limit_under;\n  wire [4:0] exp_norm     = e_norm[4:0];\n\n  // Subnormal sticky uses the original shift count.\n  wire        sticky_s   = (total_shift >= 7'd2 && total_shift < 7'd23) ? below[total_shift[4:0] - 5'd2] : 1'b0;\n  wire [9:0]  frac_trunc = shifted[10:1];\n  wire        round_s    = guard & (sticky_s | frac_trunc[0]);\n  wire [9:0]  frac_sub_inc = frac_trunc + {9'd0, round_s};\n  wire        carry_s    = round_s & (&frac_trunc);\n  wire [9:0]  frac_sub   = carry_s ? 5'd1 : e_a;\n  wire [4:0]  eb_eff = e_b_zero ? 5'd1 : e_b;\n\n  wire [PROD_W-1:0] prod = m_a * m_b;\n\n  wire [4:0]  lz;\n  wire [11:0] top = prod[21:10];\n  assign lz = top[11] ? 5'd0  :\n              top[10] ? 5'd1  :\n              top[9]  ? 5'd2  :\n              top[8]  ? 5'd3  :\n              top[7]  ? 5'd4  :\n              top[6]  ? 5'd5  :\n              top[5]  ? 5'd6  :\n              top[4]  ? 5'd7  :\n              top[3]  ? 5'd8  :\n              top[2]  ? 5'd9  :\n              top[1]  ? 5'd10 :\n              top[0]  ? 5'd11 : 5'd12;\n\n  wire [PROD_W-1:0] below;\n  assign below[0] = prod[0];\n  genvar k;\n  generate\n    for (k = 1; k < PROD_W; k = k + 1) begin : g_below\n      assign below[k] = prod[k] | below[k-1];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  wire [5:0] exp_sum = {1'b0, ea_eff} + {1'b0, eb_eff};\n\n  wire [4:0] sr = 5'd11 - lz;\n  wire [PROD_W-1:0] shifted_n;\n  assign shifted_n = (sr == 5'd0) ? 10'd0 : frac_inc;\n\n  wire [5:0] exp_c        = exp_sum + {5'd0, carry};\n  wire [5:0] limit_under  = 6'd14 + {1'b0, lz};\n  wire [5:0] limit_over   = 6'd44 + {1'b0, lz};\n  wire       underflow    = (exp_c <= limit_under);\n  wire       overflow     = (exp_c >  limit_over);\n  wire [5:0] e_norm       = exp_c - limit_under;\n  wire [4:0] exp_norm     = e_norm[4:0];\n\n  wire [6:0] total_shift = 7'd26 - {1'b0, exp_sum};\n  wire       ts_valid    = ({1'b0, exp_sum} <= 7'd25);\n  wire [4:0] ts_m1       = total_shift[4:0] - 5'd1;\n  wire [PROD_W-1:0] shifted_s = ts_valid ? (prod >> ts_m1) : {PROD_W{1'b0}};\n\n  wire [9:0]  frac_trunc = shifted_s[10:1];\n  wire        guard_s    = shifted_s[0];\n  wire        sticky_s   = (total_shift >= 7'd2 && total_shift < 7'd23) ? below[total_shift[4:0] - 5'd2] : 1'b0;\n  wire        round_s    = guard_s & (sticky_s | frac_trunc[0]);\n  wire [9:0]  frac_sub_inc = frac_trunc + {9'd0, round_s};\n  wire        carry_s    = round_s & (&frac_trunc);\n  wire [9:0]  frac_sub   = carry_s ? 10'd0 : frac_sub_inc;\n\n  wire is_nan      = nan_in;\n  wire is_inf      = ~is_nan & (inf_in | (overflow & ~zero_in));\n  wire is_sub      = ~is_nan & ~is_inf & underflow;\n  wire sub_is_zero = is_sub & ~carry_s & (frac_sub_inc == 10'd0);\n  wire is_zero     = ~is_nan & ~is_inf & (zero_in | sub_is_zero);\n\n  wire [4:0] exp_y  = (is_nan | is_inf) ? 5'd31 : is_zero ? 5'd0 : is_sub ? {4'd0, carry_s} : exp_norm;\n  wire [9:0] frac_y = is_nan ? 10'h200 : (is_inf | is_zero) ? 10'd0 : is_sub ? frac_sub : frac_norm;\n  wire       sign_y = is_nan ? 1'b0 : s_y;\n\n  assign y = {sign_y, exp_y, frac_y};\nendmodule")

The design session is now over. Final result: PASS, best cost: 1.236e+05 area_delay_product.

Please write a brief summary covering:
1. What approaches did you try and what worked best?
2. What optimizations had the most impact?
3. What didn't work or caused regressions?
4. Lessons learned and what you would do differently next time.

Your design files are carried over to the next agent, so refer to them by filename where useful.
Be concise and specific.

[ASSISTANT]
Summary:

1. **Approaches tried**  
   - Started from the provided correct `starting_point.v` and confirmed it passed (`area_delay_product ≈ 145429`).  
   - Iterated through several variants:
     - `fp_mul_v1.v`: priority-encoder-style LZC, simplified normal right shift.
     - `fp_mul_v4.v`–`v6`: combined guard/extract shifts, combinational subnormal shifter.
     - `fp_mul_v8.v`: manual rounding incrementers and compact 6-bit exponent comparisons.
     - `fp_mul_v9.v`: decomposed 10×10 significand multiply.
     - `fp_mul_v13.v`: shared normal/subnormal shifter.
     - `fp_mul_v14.v`/`v15`: ripple sticky-prefix.

2. **What worked best**  
   Final best is `fp_mul_v14.v`:
   - Correct on all 18,874,371 checks.
   - `area_delay_product = 123594.06`
   - `delay = 1209.78`, `area = 102.16`

   The main wins were:
   - Manual rounding incrementer and compact exponent comparison from `fp_mul_v8.v`.
   - Converted the final 11-bit rounding adder into a 10-bit fraction increment plus explicit carry.
   - Replaced the post-round exponent add/compare with the simpler `exp_c - (14+lz)` comparison.
   - Ripple sticky-prefix in `fp_mul_v14.v` reduced area/delay product further versus wide `|prod[k:0]` reductions.

3. **What didn’t work or caused regressions**  
   - `fp_mul_v1.v`: failed lint due to `casez`/`?` don’t-cares, but became correct after changing to priority mux LZC.
   - `fp_mul_v4.v`: initial shared shifter bug when `sr==0`; fixed by introducing the correct padded special value.
   - `fp_mul_v5.v`: too-complex shared shifter experiment was not evaluated/fully debugged.
   - `fp_mul_v7.v`: packed output muxes regressed area-delay product.
   - `fp_mul_v9.v` and `fp_mul_v10.v`: decomposed/truncated multiplier datapath was correct but not better than `v8`/`v14`.
   - `fp_mul_v13.v`: shared shifter produced a correct final result after a sticky-index fix, but delayed the path and did not beat `v14`.
   - `target_delay` changes did not alter extracted cost for the designs tried; synthesis timing remained violated unless RTL structure changed.

4. **Lessons learned / next steps**  
   - Small structural RTL changes, not `target_delay` sweeping, gave the real cost improvements.
   - Keeping the multiplier as one 11×11 product was usually better than decomposing it manually.
   - Avoid broad/wide dynamic muxes and redundant shifters; two separate shifters (`normal` and `subnormal`) were often cheaper in area-delay terms than a combined selectable one.
   - Manual carry/increment logic around rounding helped more than using explicit 11-bit adders.
   - Next steps would explore:
     - Tree-balanced shortening/truncation of the product before round/sticky extraction.
     - Lower-area rounding decision with direct sticky bits rather than full `below[k]` prefixes.
     - More aggressive packing/encoding of special case muxing.
